# Social Studies Teacher Starter Pack — Primary 1–3

**Curriculum:** Thai Basic Education Core Curriculum B.E. 2551 (2008), revised indicators where applicable
**Coverage:** 104 official indicator lessons
**Source alignment:** Each lesson retains the indicator code, learning standard and original Thai indicator wording from the curriculum database.

> **Classification note:** All objectives in this pack have an OBEC between-learning / terminal pathway classification. Where a 2021 Must-Know / Should-Know priority label is present, it is shown separately; the two classification systems are not interchangeable.

## Teaching guidance

Use the indicator as the learning target. Adapt examples to the learners' community and grade level. For religious content, respect the learner's faith and teach the curriculum's comparative language inclusively. For history and civics, distinguish evidence from opinion. For geography, use maps, local observations and reliable data. For economics, use familiar household and community examples.

## Indicator lessons

### 1. SO1.1-P1-01

- **Standard:** SO1.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P1-01` — บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card:** Use a teacher-verified short biography for the founder selected in the curriculum. Include the life stages/events required by this grade's indicator and a source note.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the founder and the first life event named in the source. This assesses: “บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป”. **Answer:** Correct founder and source detail.
2. **Explain / compare:** Put two events from the source in chronological order and cite the detail that establishes their order. This task assesses: “บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป”. **Answer:** Events are in the source's chronological order, with a supporting clue.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Summarise the required life stages in a short oral or written account using only sourced details. This task assesses: “บอกพุทธประวัติ หรือประวัติของศาสดาที่ตนนับถือโดยสังเขป”. **Answer:** Concise, accurate summary covering the indicator's required span; no invented dates or events.
### 2. SO1.1-P1-02

- **Standard:** SO1.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ชื่นชมและบอกแบบอย่างการดำเนินชีวิตและข้อคิดจากประวัติสาวก ชาดก/เรื่องเล่า และศาสนิกชนตัวอย่างตามที่กำหนด

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P1-02` — ชื่นชมและบอกแบบอย่างการดำเนินชีวิตและข้อคิดจากประวัติสาวก ชาดก/เรื่องเล่า และศาสนิกชนตัวอย่างตามที่กำหนด
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ชื่นชมและบอกแบบอย่างการดำเนินชีวิตและข้อคิดจากประวัติสาวก ชาดก/เรื่องเล่า และศาสนิกชนตัวอย่างตามที่กำหนด
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ชื่นชมและบอกแบบอย่างการดำเนินชีวิตและข้อคิดจากประวัติสาวก ชาดก/เรื่องเล่า และศาสนิกชนตัวอย่างตามที่กำหนด

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P1-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card:** Use the curriculum-specified biography, Jataka or exemplary-adherent story. Provide a short source passage identifying the person's actions and their consequences.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Name the person and identify one action stated in the source. This assesses: “ชื่นชมและบอกแบบอย่างการดำเนินชีวิตและข้อคิดจากประวัติสาวก ชาดก/เรื่องเล่า และศาสนิกชนตัวอย่างตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Correct person and source action.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain the lesson shown by that action and point to the source detail. This task assesses: “ชื่นชมและบอกแบบอย่างการดำเนินชีวิตและข้อคิดจากประวัติสาวก ชาดก/เรื่องเล่า และศาสนิกชนตัวอย่างตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Lesson is a reasonable interpretation supported by the action.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Apply the lesson to a new school or community case; recommend an action and explain why it fits. This task assesses: “ชื่นชมและบอกแบบอย่างการดำเนินชีวิตและข้อคิดจากประวัติสาวก ชาดก/เรื่องเล่า และศาสนิกชนตัวอย่างตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Feasible action explicitly linked to the lesson; accept equivalent examples.
### 3. SO1.1-P1-03

- **Standard:** SO1.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** บอกความหมาย ความสำคัญ และเคารพพระรัตนตรัย ปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P1-03` — บอกความหมาย ความสำคัญ และเคารพพระรัตนตรัย ปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บอกความหมาย ความสำคัญ และเคารพพระรัตนตรัย ปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกความหมาย ความสำคัญ และเคารพพระรัตนตรัย ปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P1-03: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card:** Use a teacher-approved concept card for the Three Jewels and Three Exhortations, or the exact corresponding teaching specified for the selected tradition. Include verified definitions and examples.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Match each term or example to its approved meaning on the card. This assesses: “บอกความหมาย ความสำคัญ และเคารพพระรัตนตรัย ปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Matches the verified card exactly.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain the meaning or importance of one selected Jewel or principle using the approved definition. This task assesses: “บอกความหมาย ความสำคัญ และเคารพพระรัตนตรัย ปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Explanation is consistent with the approved definition.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Resolve a short ethical dilemma using one principle from the card and justify the action. This task assesses: “บอกความหมาย ความสำคัญ และเคารพพระรัตนตรัย ปฏิบัติตามหลักธรรมโอวาท ๓ ในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือหลักธรรมของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Feasible action and explicit principle-based reason; never assess personal faith or worship.
### 4. SO1.1-P1-04

- **Standard:** SO1.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิต ตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P1-04` — เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิต ตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิต ตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิต ตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P1-04: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card:** Use a teacher-approved sequence card for the selected tradition's prayer, loving-kindness or mental-development practice, with steps and purpose. Offer a non-worship mindfulness observation option.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the practice and sequence its main steps from the source. This assesses: “เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิต ตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Practice and steps match the source.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain how one step supports the purpose stated in the source. This task assesses: “เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิต ตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Purpose is accurate and source-grounded.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Describe or design a brief attention/reflection routine and one observable sign that it supports focus. This task assesses: “เห็นคุณค่าและสวดมนต์ แผ่เมตตา มีสติที่เป็นพื้นฐานของสมาธิในพระพุทธศาสนาหรือการพัฒนาจิต ตามแนวทางของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Safe, voluntary routine with observable evidence; do not grade prayer, belief or spiritual attainment.
### 5. SO1.2-P1-01

- **Standard:** SO1.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บำเพ็ญประโยชน์ต่อวัด หรือศาสนสถานของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ

- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บำเพ็ญประโยชน์ต่อวัด หรือศาสนสถานของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บำเพ็ญประโยชน์ต่อวัด หรือศาสนสถานของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.2-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card:** Use a permission-cleared photo sequence or plan for a safe, age-appropriate service activity at a temple or relevant religious community place. Include adult supervision and safety guidance.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the place and one service task shown in the plan. This assesses: “บำเพ็ญประโยชน์ต่อวัด หรือศาสนสถานของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ”. **Answer:** Correct place and task.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain how the task helps care for the place or people who use it. This task assesses: “บำเพ็ญประโยชน์ต่อวัด หรือศาสนสถานของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ”. **Answer:** Practical benefit supported by the plan.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Select a safe task suitable for a child and state one permission, safety or respect rule. This task assesses: “บำเพ็ญประโยชน์ต่อวัด หรือศาสนสถานของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ”. **Answer:** Safe supervised action and relevant rule; provide an equivalent community-care alternative where participation is inappropriate.
### 6. SO1.2-P1-02

- **2021 priority classification:** should_know

- **Official indicator:** แสดงตนเป็นพุทธมามกะ หรือแสดงตนเป็นศาสนิกชนของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card:** Use a neutral, teacher-verified case about a fictional person voluntarily identifying with or joining the selected religious community. Never ask learners to disclose their own faith.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the community and formal step described in the fictional case. This assesses: “แสดงตนเป็นพุทธมามกะ หรือแสดงตนเป็นศาสนิกชนของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ”. **Answer:** Correctly identifies only what the case states.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain what the step means within the tradition described by the source. This task assesses: “แสดงตนเป็นพุทธมามกะ หรือแสดงตนเป็นศาสนิกชนของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ”. **Answer:** Explanation matches the verified source.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Explain why affiliation must be voluntary and how classmates should respect a person's choice. This task assesses: “แสดงตนเป็นพุทธมามกะ หรือแสดงตนเป็นศาสนิกชนของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ”. **Answer:** States choice must be voluntary and free from pressure or discrimination; no learner disclosure or ceremony required.
### 7. SO1.2-P1-03

- **Standard:** SO1.2
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

**Objective:** `SO1.2-P1-03` — ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.2-P1-03: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card:** Use a teacher-verified sequence/information card for one curriculum-specified ceremony, ritual or important day. Include its purpose, key actions and observer/participant conduct.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Name the event and identify or sequence its key actions from the card. This assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Event and actions are accurately identified.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain the purpose of one action using the source note. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Purpose matches the verified source.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Prepare a short guide for a respectful observer with three context-specific rules and reasons. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Rules are appropriate to this event; observing is acceptable instead of worship participation.
### 8. SO2.1-P1-01

- **Standard:** SO2.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน

**Objective:** `SO2.1-P1-01` — บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Introduce a realistic school or community situation in which a right, duty, rule or public responsibility matters. Ask who is affected.
2. Read the case and relevant rule/process together. Identify the people involved, the right or duty at stake, and the authority or process that applies.
3. Model a stakeholder chart. Groups compare possible responses for fairness, rights, responsibilities and consequences, citing the relevant rule.
4. Each learner proposes and justifies a fair, lawful next step. Check that the recommendation protects affected people and uses the relevant civic process.

**Resources:** a school/community case card, a stakeholder chart and the relevant child-rights, school-rule or civic-process extract.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน

**Practical activity:** Present a realistic school or community case; identify people involved, rights, duties, rules or civic processes, and a fair next step. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO2.1-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community case card:** A class has 4 shared storybooks, 6 learners who want to read, and one classroom rule: return a book before another learner borrows it. The teacher adds one short case detail matched to the indicator (family role, classroom duty, community decision, cultural practice, rights or public holiday).

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Read the case card. Identify the person, group, right, duty, rule, custom or decision-making step that is directly relevant to “บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน”. Give one detail from the case as evidence. **Answer:** Identify the relevant person/group/concept or step and cite the matching detail in the case. The response must fit the exact indicator, not merely repeat a general classroom rule.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain what would happen in this case if the relevant role, right, duty, custom or decision process in “บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน” were followed. Include one effect on another person or the community. **Answer:** State a plausible effect that follows from the case and correctly explains the indicator concept. Credit a clear cause-and-effect link and a relevant effect on others.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** The class must choose a fair action for the case. Propose one action, name who should take part or be responsible, and justify it using “บอกประโยชน์และปฏิบัติตนเป็นสมาชิกที่ดีของครอบครัวและโรงเรียน”. **Answer:** The proposal is practical, respects the relevant rights/duties/customs or decision process, identifies the responsible participant(s), and gives a reason connected to the indicator. Accept alternative fair solutions with sound justification.

Propose one action, name who should take part or be responsible, and justify it using “มีส่วนร่วมในการตัดสินใจและทำกิจกรรมในครอบครัวและโรงเรียนตามกระบวนการประชาธิปไตย”. **Answer:** The proposal is practical, respects the relevant rights/duties/customs or decision process, identifies the responsible participant(s), and gives a reason connected to the indicator. Accept alternative fair solutions with sound justification.
### 13. SO3.1-P1-01

- **Standard:** SO3.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ระบุสินค้า และบริการที่ใช้ประโยชน์ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Objective:** `SO3.1-P1-01` — ระบุสินค้า และบริการที่ใช้ประโยชน์ในชีวิตประจำวัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ระบุสินค้า และบริการที่ใช้ประโยชน์ในชีวิตประจำวัน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Present a familiar choice involving needs, wants, limited resources, work, goods, services or money. Ask learners what must be decided.
2. Identify the resources and constraints in the case. Model the relevant economic concept using a simple table, picture, calculation or flow diagram.
3. Pairs compare at least two choices, considering costs, benefits, opportunity, scarcity or effects on people as required by the indicator.
4. Learners complete an individual decision or recommendation and justify it with evidence from the case. Assess the economic reasoning, not merely the selected option.

**Resources:** a household, school or community decision card, a simple budget/resource table and a price, needs or production example.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ระบุสินค้า และบริการที่ใช้ประโยชน์ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Practical activity:** Use a familiar household, workplace or community decision; identify resources, choices, costs, benefits or economic relationships. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO3.1-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — simple spending card:** A learner has 20 baht. A pencil costs 5 baht, a banana costs 7 baht and a small notebook costs 8 baht. The learner wants all three items but has only 20 baht.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the numbers and details in the stimulus. Identify the good, service, resource, need, want, income, expense or saving that best demonstrates “ระบุสินค้า และบริการที่ใช้ประโยชน์ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. Show the relevant number or detail. **Answer:** The response correctly identifies the economic concept requested by the indicator and uses the exact supporting number/detail from the stimulus. For calculations, accept only mathematically correct totals and balances.
2. **Explain / compare:** Use the stimulus to explain one choice a learner or family can make that demonstrates “ระบุสินค้า และบริการที่ใช้ประโยชน์ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. Include the trade-off or result of that choice. **Answer:** The explanation names a realistic choice, uses the stimulus evidence and accurately describes its result (such as remaining money, a forgone purchase, a saved resource or a benefit to buyer/seller).
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Change one detail in the stimulus (for example, the amount of money or the available resource). Explain how the decision should change to continue meeting “ระบุสินค้า และบริการที่ใช้ประโยชน์ในชีวิตประจำวัน”, and give a reason. **Answer:** The revised decision follows logically from the changed constraint and is justified using the indicator concept. Accept more than one valid solution; check arithmetic and whether the response respects limited resources.

### 17. SO4.1-P1-01

- **Standard:** SO4.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Objective:** `SO4.1-P1-01` — บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.1-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Prepare a real calendar used by the school/community and a second teacher-verified calendar example if comparison is needed. Clearly label day, month and year; do not mix calendar systems without naming them.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Read the displayed date and state its day, month and year. This assesses: “บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** Reads all date parts correctly from the supplied calendar.
2. **Explain / compare:** Find a second date on the same calendar and explain whether it is earlier or later. This task assesses: “บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** Correctly compares dates using the same named calendar system.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Use the calendar to identify the date of a named classroom event and explain how you found it. This task assesses: “บอกวัน เดือน ปี และการนับช่วงเวลาตามปฏิทินที่ใช้ในชีวิตประจำวัน”. **Answer:** Gives the correct date and describes a valid way of locating it.
### 18. SO4.1-P1-02

- **Standard:** SO4.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** เรียงลำดับเหตุการณ์ในชีวิตประจำวัน ตามวันเวลาที่เกิดขึ้น

**Objective:** `SO4.1-P1-02` — เรียงลำดับเหตุการณ์ในชีวิตประจำวัน ตามวันเวลาที่เกิดขึ้น
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: เรียงลำดับเหตุการณ์ในชีวิตประจำวัน ตามวันเวลาที่เกิดขึ้น
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: เรียงลำดับเหตุการณ์ในชีวิตประจำวัน ตามวันเวลาที่เกิดขึ้น

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.1-P1-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Use a learner-safe set of dated daily routine cards (wake up, travel, lesson, meal, return home) with times or sequence clues.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Name the event that happens first and the event that happens last in the supplied routine. This assesses: “เรียงลำดับเหตุการณ์ในชีวิตประจำวัน ตามวันเวลาที่เกิดขึ้น”. **Answer:** Correct first and last events.
2. **Explain / compare:** Arrange four routine cards in the order supported by their times or clues. This task assesses: “เรียงลำดับเหตุการณ์ในชีวิตประจำวัน ตามวันเวลาที่เกิดขึ้น”. **Answer:** Four events are in supported chronological order.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Describe one daily event and identify what happens immediately before and after it. This task assesses: “เรียงลำดับเหตุการณ์ในชีวิตประจำวัน ตามวันเวลาที่เกิดขึ้น”. **Answer:** Identifies valid preceding and following events from the routine.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกประวัติความเป็นมาของตนเองและครอบครัวโดยสอบถามผู้เกี่ยวข้อง

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.1-P1-03: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Use a voluntary, permission-cleared family timeline or fictional family case. Let learners omit private details; label which information came from an interview and which from a document.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify one fact about the person/family from the supplied interview or document. This assesses: “บอกประวัติความเป็นมาของตนเองและครอบครัวโดยสอบถามผู้เกี่ยวข้อง”. **Answer:** Fact matches the source.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain who supplied the information and how the source supports the family account. This task assesses: “บอกประวัติความเป็นมาของตนเองและครอบครัวโดยสอบถามผู้เกี่ยวข้อง”. **Answer:** Names the source correctly and distinguishes interview from document.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Create a short family timeline using two or three approved details and label the source of each. This task assesses: “บอกประวัติความเป็นมาของตนเองและครอบครัวโดยสอบถามผู้เกี่ยวข้อง”. **Answer:** Chronology and source labels are correct; fictional or anonymised cases are acceptable.
### 20. SO4.2-P1-01

- **Standard:** SO4.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกความเปลี่ยนแปลงของสภาพแวดล้อม สิ่งของเครื่องใช้ หรือการดำเนินชีวิตของตนเองกับสมัยของพ่อแม่ ปู่ย่า ตายาย

**Objective:** `SO4.2-P1-01` — บอกความเปลี่ยนแปลงของสภาพแวดล้อม สิ่งของเครื่องใช้ หรือการดำเนินชีวิตของตนเองกับสมัยของพ่อแม่ ปู่ย่า ตายาย
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บอกความเปลี่ยนแปลงของสภาพแวดล้อม สิ่งของเครื่องใช้ หรือการดำเนินชีวิตของตนเองกับสมัยของพ่อแม่ ปู่ย่า ตายาย
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกความเปลี่ยนแปลงของสภาพแวดล้อม สิ่งของเครื่องใช้ หรือการดำเนินชีวิตของตนเองกับสมัยของพ่อแม่ ปู่ย่า ตายาย

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.2-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Use dated photographs or object cards showing one aspect of a child's life in two generations (for example transport, household tools or communication). Identify who supplied each item.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify one object or way of life in each time period. This assesses: “บอกความเปลี่ยนแปลงของสภาพแวดล้อม สิ่งของเครื่องใช้ หรือการดำเนินชีวิตของตนเองกับสมัยของพ่อแม่ ปู่ย่า ตายาย”. **Answer:** Identifies items and periods accurately.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare the two examples and state one change supported by the pictures or captions. This task assesses: “บอกความเปลี่ยนแปลงของสภาพแวดล้อม สิ่งของเครื่องใช้ หรือการดำเนินชีวิตของตนเองกับสมัยของพ่อแม่ ปู่ย่า ตายาย”. **Answer:** Names a visible, supported difference.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Explain how the change affects one everyday activity, using evidence from the set. This task assesses: “บอกความเปลี่ยนแปลงของสภาพแวดล้อม สิ่งของเครื่องใช้ หรือการดำเนินชีวิตของตนเองกับสมัยของพ่อแม่ ปู่ย่า ตายาย”. **Answer:** Explains a plausible effect tied to the evidence; avoid treating one family as representative of all families.
### 21. SO4.2-P1-02

- **Standard:** SO4.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** บอกเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในอดีตที่มีผลกระทบต่อตนเองในปัจจุบัน

**Objective:** `SO4.2-P1-02` — บอกเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในอดีตที่มีผลกระทบต่อตนเองในปัจจุบัน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บอกเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในอดีตที่มีผลกระทบต่อตนเองในปัจจุบัน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในอดีตที่มีผลกระทบต่อตนเองในปัจจุบัน

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.2-P1-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Use a verified, age-appropriate account of a past event that directly affected the learner or a fictional child/community; include dates or source attribution.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the past event and one detail that shows when or where it happened. This assesses: “บอกเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในอดีตที่มีผลกระทบต่อตนเองในปัจจุบัน”. **Answer:** Event and detail match the source.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain one effect the event had on the person/community in the source. This task assesses: “บอกเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในอดีตที่มีผลกระทบต่อตนเองในปัจจุบัน”. **Answer:** Effect is stated in the account and linked to the event.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Separate one fact from one present-day opinion about the event and explain the difference. This task assesses: “บอกเหตุการณ์ที่เกิดขึ้นในอดีตที่มีผลกระทบต่อตนเองในปัจจุบัน”. **Answer:** Fact is source-verifiable; opinion is clearly marked as interpretation.
### 22. SO4.3-P1-01

- **Standard:** SO4.3
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** อธิบายความหมายและความสำคัญของสัญลักษณ์สำคัญของชาติไทยและปฏิบัติได้ถูกต้อง

**Objective:** `SO4.3-P1-01` — อธิบายความหมายและความสำคัญของสัญลักษณ์สำคัญของชาติไทยและปฏิบัติได้ถูกต้อง
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: อธิบายความหมายและความสำคัญของสัญลักษณ์สำคัญของชาติไทยและปฏิบัติได้ถูกต้อง
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: อธิบายความหมายและความสำคัญของสัญลักษณ์สำคัญของชาติไทยและปฏิบัติได้ถูกต้อง

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.3-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Use official, current images/text for the national symbols specified in the adopted curriculum and a teacher-verified explanation of their meaning and correct respectful conduct.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Name the national symbol shown and identify one feature accurately. This assesses: “อธิบายความหมายและความสำคัญของสัญลักษณ์สำคัญของชาติไทยและปฏิบัติได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Correct symbol and feature.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain its meaning or importance using the approved information card. This task assesses: “อธิบายความหมายและความสำคัญของสัญลักษณ์สำคัญของชาติไทยและปฏิบัติได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Meaning follows the approved source.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Choose the correct respectful action for a classroom scenario involving the symbol and justify it using the guidance. This task assesses: “อธิบายความหมายและความสำคัญของสัญลักษณ์สำคัญของชาติไทยและปฏิบัติได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Action matches current official guidance; do not add unsupported rules.
### 23. SO4.3-P1-02

- **Standard:** SO4.3
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกสถานที่สำคัญซึ่งเป็นแหล่งวัฒนธรรมในชุมชน

**Objective:** `SO4.3-P1-02` — บอกสถานที่สำคัญซึ่งเป็นแหล่งวัฒนธรรมในชุมชน
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บอกสถานที่สำคัญซึ่งเป็นแหล่งวัฒนธรรมในชุมชน
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกสถานที่สำคัญซึ่งเป็นแหล่งวัฒนธรรมในชุมชน

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.3-P1-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Use a verified map/photo and official or locally authoritative description of a cultural site in the learner's community; distinguish confirmed facts from local stories.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Name the cultural place and identify where it is located on the map. This assesses: “บอกสถานที่สำคัญซึ่งเป็นแหล่งวัฒนธรรมในชุมชน”. **Answer:** Correct name and location.
2. **Explain / compare:** Use the source to explain one reason the place is important to the community. This task assesses: “บอกสถานที่สำคัญซึ่งเป็นแหล่งวัฒนธรรมในชุมชน”. **Answer:** Importance is supported by the description/source.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Suggest one respectful way to care for or learn about the place and identify the source that supports its importance. This task assesses: “บอกสถานที่สำคัญซึ่งเป็นแหล่งวัฒนธรรมในชุมชน”. **Answer:** Action is safe, respectful and appropriate to the site's rules.
### 24. SO4.3-P1-03

- **Standard:** SO4.3
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** ระบุสิ่งที่ตนรักและภาคภูมิใจในท้องถิ่น

**Objective:** `SO4.3-P1-03` — ระบุสิ่งที่ตนรักและภาคภูมิใจในท้องถิ่น
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ระบุสิ่งที่ตนรักและภาคภูมิใจในท้องถิ่น
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Display an age-appropriate historical image, object or event prompt. Ask learners what they notice and what they still need evidence to establish.
2. Model how to identify source type, author/context, date and relevant detail. Separate what the source directly shows from an inference or opinion.
3. Learners sequence events or compare two sources, recording details that support a cause, consequence, change, continuity or historical interpretation relevant to the indicator.
4. Each learner writes, explains or presents a short conclusion supported by source evidence. Check chronology and whether the evidence actually supports the claim.

**Resources:** a dated photograph or artefact image, a short primary/secondary source extract and a timeline or source-analysis frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ระบุสิ่งที่ตนรักและภาคภูมิใจในท้องถิ่น

**Practical activity:** Use an age-appropriate historical source, image or timeline; identify evidence, sequence events and distinguish evidence from opinion. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO4.3-P1-03: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — local-history evidence set:** Use a set of learner-selected or teacher-verified local objects, places, practices or community achievements. Require evidence and avoid assuming every learner feels the same attachment.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify one local feature or achievement in the evidence set. This assesses: “ระบุสิ่งที่ตนรักและภาคภูมิใจในท้องถิ่น”. **Answer:** Correctly identifies the selected local feature.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain why a person or group may value it, using one source detail. This task assesses: “ระบุสิ่งที่ตนรักและภาคภูมิใจในท้องถิ่น”. **Answer:** Gives a reason grounded in source or community testimony.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Prepare a short evidence-based caption explaining what is worth learning about or preserving locally. This task assesses: “ระบุสิ่งที่ตนรักและภาคภูมิใจในท้องถิ่น”. **Answer:** Caption is accurate and respectful; learners may choose different examples.
### 25. SO5.1-P1-01

- **Standard:** SO5.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น

**Objective:** `SO5.1-P1-01` — จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.
4. Learners produce an annotated map, short interpretation or evidence-based response. Assess correct use of geographic evidence and the link between people and place.

**Resources:** a labelled map or atlas extract, a local photograph, a simple map/data set and a geographic vocabulary frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น

**Practical activity:** Use a local map, photograph, spatial data or environmental case; identify location/pattern, explain the relationship and propose an evidence-based action. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO5.1-P1-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community geography field card:** Use a simple north-arrow sketch of the school area. Mark the classroom, gate, playground and one nearby natural feature. Add a short observation record (time, weather or visible environmental condition) relevant to this indicator. If the lesson concerns change or pollution, add a dated before/now photograph pair or a teacher-verified local example.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the field card, sketch or observation record. Identify the location, direction, physical feature, weather condition, resource or environmental change that directly answers “จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น”. Point to the map/photo/data as evidence. **Answer:** The answer identifies the correct feature or condition and refers to the correct map position, direction, photograph or observation. Direction must agree with the north arrow; claims about change must match the dated evidence.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare two marked places, observations or time points in the field card. Explain one spatial relationship, difference or effect relevant to “จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น”. **Answer:** The comparison names both items and gives one accurate relationship/difference/effect supported by the field card. Accept different examples if map direction and evidence are correct.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Propose and sketch or describe one safe action at the marked school/community location that demonstrates “จำแนกสิ่งแวดล้อมรอบตัวที่เกิดขึ้นเองตามธรรมชาติและที่มนุษย์สร้างขึ้น”. Explain what evidence would show that the action helped. **Answer:** The action is practical for the named location, addresses the indicator, is safe for children and includes an observable success measure (for example, cleaner area, correct route/map, recorded observation or reduced waste).
### 26. SO5.1-P1-02

- **Standard:** SO5.1
- **2021 priority classification:** must_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ระบุความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ และทิศของสิ่งต่าง ๆ

**Objective:** `SO5.1-P1-02` — ระบุความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ และทิศของสิ่งต่าง ๆ
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ระบุความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ และทิศของสิ่งต่าง ๆ
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a place, landscape, route or environmental issue familiar to learners. Locate it and ask what spatial information would help explain it.
2. Model how to read the map, key, scale, direction, photograph or data display needed for the indicator. Demonstrate the geographic terms accurately.
3. Pairs identify a location, pattern or relationship and use at least two visible details from the map, image or data to explain it.
4. Learners produce an annotated map, short interpretation or evidence-based response. Assess correct use of geographic evidence and the link between people and place.

**Resources:** a labelled map or atlas extract, a local photograph, a simple map/data set and a geographic vocabulary frame.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ระบุความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ และทิศของสิ่งต่าง ๆ

**Practical activity:** Use a local map, photograph, spatial data or environmental case; identify location/pattern, explain the relationship and propose an evidence-based action. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO5.1-P1-02: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — school/community geography field card:** Use a simple north-arrow sketch of the school area. Mark the classroom, gate, playground and one nearby natural feature. Add a short observation record (time, weather or visible environmental condition) relevant to this indicator. If the lesson concerns change or pollution, add a dated before/now photograph pair or a teacher-verified local example.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Use the field card, sketch or observation record. Identify the location, direction, physical feature, weather condition, resource or environmental change that directly answers “ระบุความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ และทิศของสิ่งต่าง ๆ”. Point to the map/photo/data as evidence. **Answer:** The answer identifies the correct feature or condition and refers to the correct map position, direction, photograph or observation. Direction must agree with the north arrow; claims about change must match the dated evidence.
2. **Explain / compare:** Compare two marked places, observations or time points in the field card. Explain one spatial relationship, difference or effect relevant to “ระบุความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ และทิศของสิ่งต่าง ๆ”. **Answer:** The comparison names both items and gives one accurate relationship/difference/effect supported by the field card. Accept different examples if map direction and evidence are correct.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Propose and sketch or describe one safe action at the marked school/community location that demonstrates “ระบุความสัมพันธ์ของตำแหน่ง ระยะ และทิศของสิ่งต่าง ๆ”. Explain what evidence would show that the action helped. **Answer:** The action is practical for the named location, addresses the indicator, is safe for children and includes an observable success measure (for example, cleaner area, correct route/map, recorded observation or reduced waste).

### 32. SO1.1-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO1.1
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P2-01` — บอกความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บอกความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**

2. **Explain / compare:** Explain one contribution to an individual or community using a source detail. This task assesses: “บอกความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ”. **Answer:** Supported contribution such as ethical guidance, community support, ceremony or cultural continuity.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Compare two supplied examples with one evidence-based similarity or difference. This task assesses: “บอกความสำคัญของพระพุทธศาสนา หรือศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ”. **Answer:** Accurate comparison without ranking or judging religions.

2. **Explain / compare:** Explain one turning point between them using the biography's source note. This task assesses: “สรุปพุทธประวัติ ตั้งแต่ประสูติ จนถึงการออกผนวช หรือประวัติศาสดาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Explanation matches the supplied biography; no invented motives or dates.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Reconstruct a four-event timeline covering the required span and cite one source detail per event. This task assesses: “สรุปพุทธประวัติ ตั้งแต่ประสูติ จนถึงการออกผนวช หรือประวัติศาสดาที่ตนนับถือตามที่กำหนด”. **Answer:** Chronological, complete and source-supported timeline.

- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ

**Objective:** `SO1.1-P2-07` — บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.1-P2-07: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card:** Use a verified chart for religions included in the adopted curriculum, naming each tradition, founder/central figure where applicable, recognized scripture and source.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Match each named religion to the figure (where applicable) and scripture shown in the source. This assesses: “บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ”. **Answer:** Matches only verified names and texts; do not assume all traditions frame founders identically.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain the importance or role of one selected scripture using its source note. This task assesses: “บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ”. **Answer:** Role is supported by source, such as guidance or preservation of teachings.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Compare the role of scriptures in two traditions with one accurate similarity or difference. This task assesses: “บอกชื่อศาสนา ศาสดา และความสำคัญของคัมภีร์ของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ และศาสนาอื่น ๆ”. **Answer:** Accurate, respectful comparison without ranking traditions.
### 39. SO1.2-P2-01

- **Standard:** SO1.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** between_learning
- **Official indicator:** ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

**Objective:** `SO1.2-P2-01` — ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง
- **Learning intention:** Learners will demonstrate: ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง
**Suggested teaching sequence**

1. Begin with a familiar situation involving kindness, honesty, responsibility or respectful coexistence; invite learners to describe the action without disclosing personal beliefs.
2. Present a short, balanced source about the relevant tradition, teaching or practice. Identify its context and distinguish factual information from personal opinion.
3. Model how the source connects to the indicator. In pairs, compare two possible actions and explain which better demonstrates the stated principle while respecting different beliefs.
4. Learners complete the application task independently and explain the evidence behind their choice. Assess understanding and respectful reasoning, never personal faith or worship.

**Resources:** a short age-appropriate account, a neutral information card and a real-life ethical situation.

**Differentiation:** For early primary learners: Use large pictures, concrete objects, short oral instructions and teacher modelling; allow learners to point, sort, draw or answer orally.

**Learning intention:** Learners will be able to demonstrate the specific knowledge or performance in this official indicator: ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

**Practical activity:** Use a respectful account or case from an appropriate tradition; connect the relevant teaching, value or practice to a real-life choice without assessing personal belief. Adjust the complexity to early primary learners and use a source or response format appropriate to their age.

**Expected evidence:** Three completed responses demonstrate SO1.2-P2-01: accurate identification, a clear explanation with a relevant example, and a grade-appropriate application task. Accept oral, written, visual, map-based, timeline-based or practical evidence where it measures the same target.

**Teacher check:** Check separately (1) accuracy against the Thai indicator, (2) explanation supported by an example or source, and (3) application to the scenario. Give feedback on the missing criterion.

**Assessment stimulus — verified tradition/source card:** Use verified case cards for respectful conduct toward adherents, religious places and religious objects of the selected tradition; include context-specific instructions.

**Independent practice — indicator-specific assessment tasks**
1. **Recall / evidence:** Identify the person, place or object in each case and select the respectful action. This assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Correct identification and action.
2. **Explain / compare:** Explain why one action is appropriate, citing the case instruction. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Reason is grounded in the source/context.
3. **Apply / demonstrate:** Create a visitor checklist with three practical rules for the represented site or interaction. This task assesses: “ปฏิบัติตนอย่างเหมาะสมต่อสาวกของศาสนาที่ตนนับถือ ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง”. **Answer:** Three practical, context-specific rules; avoid assuming all traditions share identical practices.
### 40. SO1.2-P2-02

- **Standard:** SO1.2
- **2021 priority classification:** should_know
- **OBEC pathway classification:** terminal
- **Official indicator:** ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง

**Objective:** `SO1.2-P2-02` — ปฏิบัติตนในศาสนพิธี พิธีกรรม และวันสำคัญทางศาสนา ตามที่กำหนดได้ถูกต้อง
